- **2025–26 change:** 1) July 1, 2025 (announced June 27, 2025): the classic Founders Hub multi-tier program (apply-then-upgrade to $100 → $5,000 → $25,000 → $150,000 with business-plan validation) was RETIRED and replaced by a two-track system: Investor Network track ($100,000+ starting credits, referral from affiliated investors/accelerators) and a self-serve track ($1,000 for 90 days + $4,000 for 180 days after business verification, cap $5,000 without investor affiliation). Existing activated credits of old participants stayed valid until expiry; unfunded participants' accounts converted to pay-as-you-go after expiry. Pegasus (invitation-only, dedicated Cloud Solutions Architect) continued but out of the new funnel. 2) 2026 restructure (MFS 2.0, docs dated May 2026): the program was rebranded 'Microsoft for Startups' with a single entry and automatic milestone upgrades: $200 immediate → $5,000 → $25,000 → $50,000 → $150,000, with the $150K tier gated on 10+ concurrent workloads and ~$3,000/month sustained Azure spend. Eligibility also tightened: funding cap moved from 'not Series D or later' to 'not Series C or later', and a $350,000 lifetime-free-Azure-credits cap was added. So the oft-quoted 'no funding required, up to $150K' is now true only in the narrow sense that early stages need no funding, but Series C+ companies are excluded and $150K requires heavy sustained Azure spend. 3) Legacy partner credits from the old Founders Hub ($2,500 OpenAI, Databricks, Twilio $10,000, Atlassian, etc.) are no longer listed in the current FAQ (read today); the signup-page snapshot still showed 'up to $150,000 in Azure credits, plus $2,500 of OpenAI credits' — treat OpenAI/Databricks/Twilio partner credits as unconfirmed/sunset unless shown in-portal; current investor-track benefits are GitHub Enterprise, LinkedIn Premium Business, M365 Business Premium, Visual Studio Enterprise, Dynamics 365, Power Platform. 4) GitHub Student Developer Pack: DigitalOcean's $200 credit offer left the pack (credits expire 2026-08-01); Bump.sh offer ended 2025-12-09; Appwrite restructured to 6 months (2026-10-02); GitHub Foundations certification voucher was paused 2025-10-25 then resumed 2025-09-30. 5) The Azure Free page no longer advertises the old '6 months free AI services' window — only 12-month free amounts and always-free tiers.
- **Critic correction** ($100 in Azure credits, 12 months of Visual Studio Pro): The $100 credit is valid for 12 months. Visual Studio Professional is not in the included product list, which names Visual Studio Code, Visual Studio Community, Visual Studio for Mac, Agents for Visual Studio, Remote Tools for Visual Studio and Visio Professional. The 12-month Cloud Academy benefit comes with paid Visual Studio Professional subscriptions, not Azure for Students.

### Oracle Startup Growth Engine

- **Oracle Startup Growth Engine — OCI credits (LatAm startup program)** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — Up to US$60,000 in Oracle Cloud Infrastructure credits; program rules allow US$5,000–US$100,000 at Oracle's discretion, for VC/accelerator-backed, high-growth startups with a validated product. Launched June 2026 for Brazil, Argentina, Colombia with LatAm expansion planned. NOT verifiable today on public oracle.com pages: the global (oracle.com/cloud/oracle-for-startups/), LatAm, and Brazil startup pages read on 2026-10-22 contain no Growth Engine, credit-amount, VC/accelerator, or application content — they are generic 'cloud for startups' landing pages. The program front is likely a gated/partner portal.
  - *Eligibility:* VC- or accelerator-funded high-growth startup with a validated product; LatAm at launch (Brazil/Argentina/Colombia); no .edu requirement; no published startup-age or funding-cap figure on any public page read today.
  - *How to claim:* VC/accelerator partner nominates portfolio startup → startup applies through the program front (Startup Cloud-Credit Programs, VC/Accelerator Funded) → Oracle reviews product validation → credits awarded at Oracle's discretion (US$5k–US$100k band, up to US$60k) and applied to an OCI tenancy; spend on eligible OCI services.
  - *Expiry:* Not published on any public page read today; credit-consumption window unverified.
  - *Gotchas:* As of 2026-10-22 the program has zero public footprint on oracle.com global/LatAm/BR startup pages (all checked today) — expect partner/VC-gated application, not self-serve signup; award size is discretionary; these credits are separate from and in addition to the US$300 free-tier trial; WebSearch was unavailable this session, so no independent announcement could be located.
  - *Source:* https://www.oracle.com/cloud/oracle-for-startups/
- **OCI Free Trial — US$300 credits / 30 days** (high) — US$300 of free credit usable on all eligible OCI services for up to 30 days; consumption at discounted IaaS-equivalent rates during the 30-day promo; ends when credits are exhausted or 30 days elapse, whichever comes first; unused credits automatically expire. One trial per person. 'Oracle Cloud Promotion' is Oracle's alias for this same Free Trial.
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone signing up for an OCI account (FAQ explicitly names students, academics, developers, startup founders); no .edu requirement; available in all regions where commercial OCI is offered, but the US$300 credit is 'available in select countries' per the official footnote; credit or debit card (credit-like) IS required at signup.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up via signup.cloud.oracle.com (linked as 'Sign Up for Free Cloud Tier' from oracle.com/cloud/free/) → provide contact + card info for identity verification → tenancy created with US$300/30-day credit → optionally upgrade to Pay As You Go at any time via Console Account Management.
  - *Expiry:* 30 days or credit exhaustion; then a 30-day grace period to upgrade, after which trial (paid) resources and data are permanently reclaimed/deleted; Oracle sales reps can extend trials or add credits on request; upgrade during trial defers billing until credits/30 days run out; no downgrade after upgrading.
  - *Gotchas:* CC required — 'credit card NOT required' is false for this offer; virtual/single-use/prepaid and PIN debit cards are rejected; Oracle may place temporary authorization holds (3–5 days, no charge); no SLAs; only 'limited support' during trial — full support requires paid upgrade; paid instances created during trial are reclaimed when trial ends; some special trial offers suspend the account immediately after expiry (30-day suspension, then account deletion); multiple free accounts prohibited; idle 30+ days risks suspension.
  - *Source:* https://www.oracle.com/cloud/free/faq/
- **OCI Always Free services — unlimited duration (incl. for students)** (high) — Unlimited-time Always Free services (per official FAQ read today): HeatWave, AMD-based Compute, Ampere A1 Arm-based Compute, Block Volume, Object Storage, Archive Storage, Flexible + Flexible Network Load Balancer, Site-to-Site VPN/VPN Connect, Autonomous Data Warehouse/Transaction Processing/JSON Database, NoSQL Database (Phoenix region only), APEX, Resource Manager (Terraform), Monitoring, Notifications, Logging, APM, Service Connector Hub, Vault, Bastions, Security Advisor, VCNs, Content Management Starter Edition, Email Delivery. Numeric limits published on Oracle's LatAm OCI landing page (read today): 2 Autonomous AI Database instances of 20GB each; AMD and Arm compute VMs; 200GB total block storage; 10GB object storage; 10TB/month egress. Global free/FAQ page does not print per-service OCPU/RAM caps today ('Some limitations apply').
  - *Eligibility:* Anyone, explicitly including students and academics (one account per person); no .edu required; card required at signup (identity verification); NoSQL free only in Phoenix region.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up for the same Free Tier (signup.cloud.oracle.com) → Always Free resources usable immediately and independently of trial credits → keep them after trial ends by staying within tenancy limits.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry for in-limit Always Free resources ('unlimited time'); tenancy subject to abandoned-account rules (idle 30+ days may be suspended); new Always Free services are added automatically.
  - *Gotchas:* The 10GB object-storage / 10TB egress / 200GB block figures come from the LatAm landing page while the global free page omits numbers — verify per region before relying on them; if you provision more Ampere A1 instances than the Always Free tenancy allows, ALL existing Ampere instances are disabled and then deleted 30 days post-trial unless you upgrade; data egress beyond the free monthly amount and anything outside Always Free limits is billable; no SLA/support on Always Free (community forums only); capacity estimates can sell out ('out of host capacity' errors); Always Free status is not transferable between resources.
  - *Source:* https://www.oracle.com/cloud/free/faq/
- **Oracle University free training & accreditation (education offer)** (high) — Oracle University (oracle.com/education, read today): 'Start learning for free' — free entry-level 'Foundations' training and certifications across Oracle Cloud Infrastructure, Oracle Fusion Cloud Applications, and AI; free Oracle University Learning Community (3.5M+ learners); free hands-on labs that run in free lab environments or in Oracle Cloud free mode; Oracle MyLearn AI-personalized learning. This is a training/accreditation offer, not cloud credits or a software license.
  - *Eligibility:* Open to individuals and organizations; no .edu requirement stated on the page; no fee for the free Foundations tracks.
  - *How to claim:* Go to oracle.com/education → 'Start learning for free' / 'Explore free training and certifications' → join the Learning Community → complete free Foundations courses/accreditations; run free labs in Oracle-provided free lab environments or your free-tier tenancy.
  - *Expiry:* No expiry stated; free training catalog appears ongoing.
  - *Gotchas:* Advanced role-based tracks and the Oracle Unlimited Learning Subscription are paid; free training does not include free cloud capacity (resources still count against Free Tier/trial limits).
  - *Source:* https://www.oracle.com/education/

### Startup Grad

- **Microsoft for Startups — self-serve offer (no investor code)** (high) — Milestone-based Azure Startup credits, current structure per official docs dated May–Oct 2026: $200 starter credit immediately (LinkedIn identity check); after approval (~3 business days) $1,000 in credits valid 90 days; +$4,000 valid 180 days after business verification = $5,000 total at Milestone 2. Unlocks without separate applications: $25,000 (use 5+ Azure workloads, ~60 days sustained), $50,000 (7+ workloads, ~60 days), $150,000 (10+ workloads AND ~$3,000/month usage over ~60 days; each workload must stay >$1 spend for the full 60-day window; non-sequential skips allowed). One-time 2-year extension to use credits at the $25K milestone. Credits apply to eligible Azure services including Azure AI / Microsoft Foundry models.
  - *Eligibility:* Startup (or founder 'with an idea'): privately held for-profit, software-based product/service owned by the company, pre-seed through Series B / not Series C-or-later funding, HQ in a country where Microsoft for Startups operates, lifetime free Azure credits received <$350,000, not an educational institution/government/consultancy/agency, no cryptocurrency mining. Sign-in must use a personal consumer Microsoft account (work/school/company-domain emails rejected); phone number required. No .edu requirement.
  - *How to claim:* startups.microsoft.com (or microsoft.com/en/startups 'Get started now') → sign in with personal MS account → 'Continue without a code' → Verify with LinkedIn (personal, not business) → create Azure account (profile, billing address, payment card) → $200 unlocked → decision email in ~3 business days → $1,000 → complete business verification → $5,000. Credits viewable in Azure Portal > Cost Management + Billing > Payment methods. If declined: reapply after 14 days.
  - *Expiry:* Credits valid up to 2 years once activated (extensions not available except the one-time 2-year extension at the $25K milestone). Credits must be activated within 90 days of accepting the program agreement or the offer lapses and you must reapply. Subscription auto-converts to pay-as-you-go when credits are exhausted or expired. Cannot transfer credits between Microsoft accounts/subscriptions.
  - *Gotchas:* Credit card IS required (identity verification, uncovered services, PAYG transition). Services not covered by credits, egress, or usage on a different billing profile still bill while credits remain. 'Already redeemed' error if your MSA previously redeemed another Microsoft program's credits. Country/region chosen at account creation cannot be changed later. The headline 'up to $150,000' is end-state: an unfunded solo startup realistically lands at $5,000 after verification. Trial-vs-free: this is a program, not a trial, but the first $200/$1,000 are time-boxed trial-style credits.
  - *Source:* https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/startups/microsoft-for-startups/application(also/getting-started-mfsand/mfs-faqs)
- **Microsoft for Startups — Investor Network offer (referral code; YC/VC/accelerator front)** (high) — With a 10-character referral code from a Microsoft for Startups Investor Network partner (VC, accelerator, incubator, angel network, university), most startups begin with $100,000 in Azure credits 'with the opportunity to earn more', up to $150,000. Includes Azure Standard Support, technical expert access, go-to-market support, and free/discounted tools: GitHub Enterprise, LinkedIn Premium Business, Microsoft 365 Business Premium, Visual Studio Enterprise, Dynamics 365, Power Platform; higher levels add unlimited Azure Pairing consultations and Expert Network access plus third-party benefits.
  - *Eligibility:* Same company criteria as self-serve (privately held, for-profit, software-based, not Series C or later, <$350K lifetime free Azure credits, HQ in an MfS-supported region) PLUS a valid referral code; YC is an explicit partner — YC and M12 were first to access Microsoft's free GPU cluster preview (Nov 2023), and YC is among organizations that can supply codes. Microsoft does not publish the list of participating investors.
  - *How to claim:* Ask your VC/accelerator (e.g., YC) for the 10-character code → startups.microsoft.com → enter code → verify contact email + phone → business info must exactly match legal documents (mismatches fail verification) → application review ~3 business days → $1,000 starter credits immediately → approval unlocks expanded credits/benefits. Alternatively 'Sign up here' via the investor-referral link on microsoft.com/en/startups.
  - *Expiry:* Same mechanics as self-serve: credits valid up to 2 years once activated; activation due within 90 days of acceptance; PAYG after exhaustion.
  - *Gotchas:* Benefits often cannot be redeemed on an existing enterprise account or on an account that previously redeemed the same benefit. YC-affiliated founders without YC's code only get the self-serve track. Business verification failure stalls the high-value tier. Credits locked to one MSA.
  - *Source:* https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/startups/microsoft-for-startups/mfs-faqs;https://partner.microsoft.com/en-us/partnership/founders-hub-benefits-guide
- **YC / M12 free Azure GPU clusters (Founders Hub GPU program)** (medium) — Free Azure AI infrastructure: high-end GPU virtual machine clusters for training/running LLMs and deep learning, delivered as a private preview. Announced 8 Nov 2023 with Y Combinator's cohort first (M12 portfolio next); 11 Jan 2024 Microsoft extended it to AI Grant Fund, Alchemist Accelerator, Conviction, The House Fund, OpenAI Startup Fund, Neo, and Elad Gil. This is the 'GPUs' element of the 2025–2026 successor framing to Startup Grad.
  - *Eligibility:* YC/M12 startups: limited preview cohort via the accelerator relationship — not a general self-serve application. Extension partners: founders 'affiliated' with the listed organizations.
  - *How to claim:* No public self-serve claim: access flows through the YC/investor relationship and Founders Hub application (referral-code path above).
  - *Expiry:* Described as private preview; no public sunset date published. Availability may have changed since the 2024 announcements (unverified in 2026).
  - *Gotchas:* Cohort-based and capacity-limited; GPU access is separate from Azure credit tiers; 'free' applies to the preview infrastructure, not necessarily to all surrounding egress/storage. Do not assume a YC alum can self-claim in 2026 — confirm with YC/Microsoft.
  - *Source:* https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/startups/blog/startups-to-access-high-performance-azure-infrastructure-accelerating-ai-breakthroughs/;https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/startups/blog/microsoft-expands-free-azure-ai-infrastructure-access-to-startups/
- **Azure free account — $200 trial credit + 12-month free services** (high) — $200 credit usable on Azure services within 30 days; 12 months of free monthly amounts for 20+ popular services (750 hrs each of B1s/B2pts v2/B2ats v2 VMs; 750 hrs PostgreSQL/MySQL Flexible Server B1MS with 32 GB storage+backup; 720 vCore-hrs SQL Managed Instance; 1 ACR Standard registry with 100 GB; 1M Function requests/mo; etc.); plus 65+ always-free services with free monthly amounts (Cosmos DB 1,000 RU/s + 25 GB, App Service 10 web apps, Container Apps 180k vCPU-seconds, etc.), which continue after 12 months at their monthly limits.
  - *Eligibility:* New Azure customers; one free-account offer per person; phone number and a credit card or non-prepaid debit card required (credit card only in HK/Brazil); Microsoft or GitHub account.
  - *How to claim:* azure.microsoft.com/en-us/free → 'Try Azure for free' → 3-step account creation (profile/country, billing address, payment card) → $200 usable for 30 days.
  - *Expiry:* $200 credit expires at 30 days (unused credit persists only within the 30-day window); 12-month free service amounts end at 12 months → pay-as-you-go for overages; always-free service amounts continue indefinitely.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial vs free: the 'free forever' services keep their monthly caps after the trial; 'spending protection' means the card is not charged while the offer is active (a temporary $1 authorization may post and reverse), not that no card is needed. After expiry the sponsorship subscription auto-converts to PAYG with no commitment. Egress/data beyond free amounts is billed. A .edu email does NOT upgrade this offer (student Azure access comes via the GitHub Pack instead).
  - *Source:* https://azure.microsoft.com/en-us/free/
- **GitHub Student Developer Pack (incl. Azure for students, no credit card)** (high) — Free GitHub Pro while a student; GitHub Copilot for verified students (unlimited completions + AI-credit allowance, limited chat/agent); free Pro-level GitHub Codespaces; GitHub Pages (one user site); free domain offers (Namecheap .me + SSL 1 yr, Name.com free domain, .TECH 1 yr). Microsoft Azure track for 18+: 25+ Azure services free plus $100 Azure credit, explicitly 'no credit card required'. Track for ages 13–17: limited services (App Services, Functions, Notification Hubs, MySQL, Application Insights, Azure DevOps). Other partner benefits: Heroku (24 months), Datadog Pro (2 years), JetBrains, DataCamp, Notion, MongoDB, 1Password, ~50 more.
  - *Eligibility:* Current enrolled students (verified via .edu/school email or manual GitHub enrollment verification); separate age bands 18+ vs 13–17 for the Azure offers; the Azure portion is 'no credit card required', unlike the general free account.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up at education.github.com/pack on a GitHub account, verify student status, then redeem each partner offer (Azure credit via the Azure redemption inside the pack).
  - *Expiry:* Benefits last while student status is verified; individual partner offers carry their own time boxes (e.g., domains 1 yr, Heroku 24 mo, Datadog 2 yr).
  - *Gotchas:* Redeeming the pack's Azure offer can make an MSA 'already redeemed' for other Microsoft program credits (see MFS FAQ). $100 is small — pair with MFS self-serve for more. Free Pro/Copilot stop when you're no longer a student. No explicit 2-year duration stated for the core pack itself.
  - *Source:* https://education.github.com/pack
- **Microsoft 365 A1 for Education + limited-time college Premium offer** (high) — Free 'A1' plan (donated by Microsoft): web Outlook, Word, Excel, PowerPoint, OneNote plus PC-only Publisher and Access for educators/students of qualified K-12 and higher-ed institutions; no OneDrive quota or desktop apps stated on the page. Limited-time college offer: 12 months of Microsoft 365 Premium free using a college email. Paid institutional tiers for contrast: A3 ($3.25 faculty/$2.50 student per user/mo), A5 ($8/$6).
  - *Eligibility:* .edu/institutional email required — 'working at, or enrolled in, a qualified educational organization' (K-12 and higher ed); college Premium offer requires a college email. No credit card.
  - *How to claim:* microsoft.com/en-us/education/products/office → 'Get started for students' (or 'for institutions'); automated email verification is usually instant, manual institution checks can take up to a month. College Premium: sign up with college email.
  - *Expiry:* A1 lasts while enrolled/employed; Premium college offer lasts 12 months, after which apps drop to reduced-functionality (view-only) mode.
  - *Gotchas:* A1 is web-centric: no full desktop suite and no stated 1 TB OneDrive. If the institution's plan lapses, existing files become view-only. 'Premium on us' is limited-time and regional — confirm availability.
  - *Source:* https://www.microsoft.com/en-us/education/products/office
- **Visual Studio free tiers (Community, VS Code, Dev Essentials)** (high) — Visual Studio Community edition (current VS18-generation IDE) free for individual developers on Windows; VS Code free on Windows/macOS/Linux; 'Visual Studio Dev Essentials' bundle offering free tools, Pluralsight training, and Azure credit for free. This is the surviving student IDE path: the dedicated 'Visual Studio for Students' download no longer exists (visualstudio.microsoft.com/visual-studio-for-students/ now returns 404 and the free-developer-offers page lists no student-specific program), consistent with Microsoft's 2025 shift to free IDE licensing for individuals.
  - *Eligibility:* Any individual developer, students included; no credit card; Dev Essentials is free via devessentials.microsoft.com. Community edition may not be used as an enterprise tool in large organizations (standard MS license terms).
  - *How to claim:* visualstudio.microsoft.com/free-developer-offers/ → download Community or VS Code; Dev Essentials registration for the bundle + Azure credit.
  - *Expiry:* Licensing is ongoing while free; no time-boxed expiry for Community/VS Code. Dev Essentials Azure credit has its own (small, unspecified) duration.
  - *Gotchas:* Trial-vs-free: 'Visual Studio 2022 for individual developers' is now a permanent free edition, not a trial; VS Enterprise/for Teams are paid. If you specifically need the 'Visual Studio for Students' SKU, it is gone — students should use Community or VS Code.
  - *Source:* https://visualstudio.microsoft.com/free-developer-offers/
- **Azure OpenAI / Microsoft Foundry models — free rate-limited quota tier** (medium) — Microsoft Foundry (Azure OpenAI) model quotas now run on seven tiers: Free Tier plus Tiers 1–6 with automatic upgrades based on usage/payment history. Free quota tables published per model (e.g., current docs: gpt-5 GlobalStandard ~1,000 RPM / 250,000 TPM; gpt-4o-mini GlobalStandard ~20,000 RPM / 2,000,000 TPM in the lowest tier; several models show 'N/A' for the Azure-for-Students/free-trials column, meaning no special free quota). MFS participants get model access 'through Microsoft Foundry' inside their startup credits.
  - *Eligibility:* Any Azure subscription; free tier available to new/free-trial accounts; no .edu requirement and no credit-card requirement beyond the underlying Azure account (which does need a card for the standard free account — the card-free student path is the GitHub Pack offer).
  - *How to claim:* Create an Azure resource for Foundry/OpenAI and select the free deployment tier; MFS startups simply use their startup credits on the same Foundry endpoints.
  - *Expiry:* Rate limits, not a dollar allowance; the page documents no hard monthly token cap or end date for the free tier today (older 90-day time limit unverified on the current page); auto tier-upgrades can be opted out of (NoAutoUpgrade flag).
  - *Gotchas:* Rate limits can throttle heavy dev use; model availability is region-dependent; free-tier endpoints may exclude data retention guarantees paid tiers get; 'free tier' ≠ the $200 trial credit — it coexists with pay-as-you-go billing once limits are hit.
  - *Source:* https://learn.microsoft.com/en-us/azure/foundry/openai/quotas-limits
- **2025–26 change:** 1) Startup Grad (Microsoft x YC, historical ~$50,000 Azure credits per YC company) is no longer offered: superseded by Microsoft for Startups Founders Hub (up to $150K via milestones/Investor Network, verified on official docs dated May–Oct 2026) plus the 8 Nov 2023 YC/M12 free GPU-cluster preview (extended 11 Jan 2024 to other incubators). The ~$50K figure could not be re-verified on any official Microsoft page today (secondary sources only), so the historical detail is low-confidence. 2) Major 2025 change (reported 2 Feb 2026 by theainewsdigest; corroborated by the current official docs' milestone structure): on 2 Jul 2025 Microsoft restructured Microsoft for Startups — the old four-level grid ($1K/$5K/$25K/$100K-up-to-$150K, where Level 1 gave every approved startup $1,000) was replaced by the milestone ladder (self-serve $200 → $5K → $25K → $50K → $150K) plus an Investor Network referral-code track (most investor-backed startups start at $100K). Unfunded startups effectively dropped from up to $150K to $5K total. Eligibility also shifted: lifetime-free-credit cap raised from $10K to $350K, and the funding ceiling moved from pre-Series D to pre-Series C. 3) 'Visual Studio for Students' appears sunset: its URL now 404s and the free-developer-offers page (fetched 2026-10-22) lists no student program; VS Community/VS Code are the free path (consistent with MS's 2025 move to free individual IDE licensing). 4) YC's 'successor' claim in the brief — 'up to $150K + AI Foundry + GPUs' — verified: up to $150K milestones on MFS docs, Microsoft Foundry model access inside MFS benefits, and the YC-first GPU cluster program (status in 2026 unconfirmed). Web search budget was exhausted mid-task; all 2026 items above were read directly from official pages today, except the 2 Jul 2025 restructuring (secondary source, corroborated by current docs).

### Techstars

- **Techstars accelerator investment: $220K day-one (APAC: $120K)** (high) — $220,000 invested at acceptance in most programs worldwide: $200,000 via uncapped MFN SAFE (no valuation cap, no discount; auto-adopts the lowest-cap or most-favorable terms of any SAFE issued between MFN start and the priced round) + $20,000 via post-money Convertible Equity Agreement (CEA) for 5% common stock (post-money, incl. existing option pool). Wired within 3 weeks of acceptance; offer issued 1-2 weeks after acceptance with standardized docs. Both instruments convert when the company closes a priced round of at least $1M. Asia-Pacific programs: $100K MFN SAFE + $20K CEA = $120K total. 'No fee (i.e., monetary cost) to join a Techstars accelerator program' — official. Total package advertised as $1.5M+ including credits. 3-month mentorship program; network access 'for the life of the company'.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups only (no .edu email or student requirement); idea-stage OK ('we've accepted companies with no more than an idea, and a dream') up to companies already holding $1M+ funding. Founded anywhere; must be legally present in program country during the 3-month program. Must be a US C-corp or foreign equivalent; companies incorporated in non-approved jurisdictions (e.g., India) need a 'flip' reorg first. Pre-Series A seed stage in practice. Applications via apply.techstars.com for a specific program; acceptance is competitive.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Apply to a specific Techstars program at apply.techstars.com (application takes ~30 min). 2) On acceptance: upload pitch deck, answer questions; accelerator offer + MFN SAFE term sheet issued within 1-2 weeks. 3) Complete Techstars' checks (background, incorporation, ownership, IP, company bank account) — $20K wired as soon as checks pass; legal close of standardized MFN SAFE docs in <=3 weeks. 4) $220K wired to company account; partner cloud credits activated across AWS/DigitalOcean/Google platforms at the same step.
  - *Expiry:* SAFE + CEA have no expiry; they convert on the first priced round of $1M+. Ongoing side-letter shareholder relationship (pro-rata, digital-assets, drag-along rights; company must report key operating metrics and burn). Cloud/AI credits: 24 months per Techstars' capital page (see separate findings).
  - *Gotchas:* Not a grant: $20K is equity, $200K is a convertible instrument — Techstars' future ownership = 5% + SAFE conversion value (e.g., $200K converts to 1% extra on a $20M pre-money round per official example). Uncapped MFN means if you later issue a better-term SAFE (lower cap or 20% discount), Techstars automatically inherits it. Side letter imposes reporting and exit obligations. APAC deals are only $120K. $20K/5% is structured as a CEA (converts at the priced round), though the marketing /capital page describes it as '5% common stock for $20,000'.
  - *Source:* https://www.techstars.com/capital;https://www.techstars.com/investment-terms;https://www.techstars.com/newsroom/investment-terms
- **AWS Activate — Techstars portfolio tier: $100,000 AWS credits** (medium) — $100,000 in AWS promotional credits over 24 months, 'PORTFOLIO' tier, per Techstars' official capital page (lists H100, SageMaker, EC2; infrastructure access). Zero equity taken by AWS. Techstars has been a long-standing AWS Activate Provider (a ~10-year partnership per Amazon). 2026 notes from AWS: Founders tier starts at $1,000; Portfolio tier can be up to $200,000 depending on provider; credits now redeemable on third-party models in Amazon Bedrock; new AWS accounts start on a Free plan that is NOT eligible for Activate credits (must be on Paid plan); eligibility = pre-Series B, founded within last 10 years, functioning website, business email on company domain, most recent funding round within 12 months.
  - *Eligibility:* Requires Techstars acceptance (or an AWS Activate Provider Org ID — each Org ID usable once, and AWS does the final approval above the $5K tier). No .edu email requirement. Global. Startup-stage constraint: pre-Series B per AWS rules.
  - *How to claim:* For Techstars companies: nothing to do separately — credits are 'activated across all partner platforms' at the capital-wire step after acceptance (Step 3 of Techstars' capital deployment). Standalone: apply at the AWS Activate portal (aws.amazon.com/activate) with your provider's Organization ID; decision in 5-10 business days.
  - *Expiry:* 24 months from activation per Techstars' capital page (AWS generally states 1-2 year validity and now enforces expiry strictly with no extensions).
  - *Gotchas:* Credits are promotional dollars on a paid-plan AWS account — overage beyond the credits is billed; AWS Marketplace third-party software, Route 53 domain registrations, and some support tiers are excluded. Free-plan AWS accounts cannot receive Activate credits. The AWS Techstars partnership page (aws.amazon.com/startups/partnerships/techstars → startups.aws.com) returned 404 today, so the $100K/24-month figure rests on Techstars' own official page; AWS may grant less than $100K at its discretion (the partner 'recommends' a tier, AWS approves).
  - *Source:* https://www.techstars.com/capital;https://aws.amazon.com/activate;https://www.aboutamazon.com/news/aws/how-aws-activate-program-works
- **DigitalOcean Startups — HATCH tier: $100,000 credits (2026 premier partner)** (medium) — $100,000 in DigitalOcean infrastructure credits over 24 months, 'HATCH' tier, per Techstars' official capital page (lists H100 GPU access). DigitalOcean is a former Techstars portfolio company and was welcomed in March 2026 as a 2026 'Premier Partner': Techstars founders gain access to DO's Gradient AI Platform and the DO startup community. DO's own program is tiered (historically HATCH ~$100K / SPARK ~$25K, ~12 months, 1-2% equity on HATCH, 0% on SPARK); the Techstars deal line states 24 months.
  - *Eligibility:* Requires Techstars acceptance (or a qualifying accelerator/VC partner path). No .edu email requirement. Global. Early-stage startup.
  - *How to claim:* For Techstars companies: no separate application — HATCH credits are activated at the same capital-deployment step as the AWS/Google credits after acceptance. Standalone: apply through the DigitalOcean Startups program with a partner reference.
  - *Expiry:* 24 months per Techstars' capital page (DO's standard tiers historically ~12 months — confirm current tier at claim time).
  - *Gotchas:* Credit-card-free access to DO itself: DO Startups tiers do not take meaningful equity on HATCH beyond a small % and SPARK is equity-free; DO pages are currently unreachable via the standard partner URLs (digitalocean.com/partners/startups 404 today), so the 24-month/HATCH details rest on Techstars' official page plus the March 2026 premier-partner announcement. Unused credit expires; overage beyond credits is billed on the DO account.
  - *Source:* https://www.techstars.com/capital;https://www.techstars.com/blog/impact/techstars-update-march-2026
- **Google Cloud for Startups — via Techstars: $100,000 GCP credits** (medium) — $100,000 in Google Cloud credits over 24 months, 'FOUNDERS_PROGRAM' tier, per Techstars' official capital page (lists A100, GKE, Vertex AI, Cloud GPUs). Google co-hosts Techstars investor events (e.g., Fall 2025 NYC Investor Day hosted by Google for Startups). Google's own startup portal has been reorganized (cloud.google.com/launch now 404s; program now under the 'Google Cloud for Startups' umbrella) — Google's publicly cited figures are up to $200K credits generally, with partner-accelerator cohorts getting the top package; Techstars' deal line is $100K/24 months.
  - *Eligibility:* Requires Techstars acceptance (or Google's accelerator-partner path). No .edu email requirement. Typical GCP startup program rules: early-stage (<5 years, funding/revenue caps per Google's current terms — not re-verifiable today as the program pages 404).
  - *How to claim:* For Techstars companies: no separate application — GCP credits activated at the capital-wire step after acceptance, alongside AWS/DigitalOcean. Standalone: apply via the Google Cloud for Startups portal or through a partner (Techstars, YC, 500 Global, Antler).
  - *Expiry:* 24 months per Techstars' capital page (GCP credits historically 12 months at claim; confirm at activation).
  - *Gotchas:* Google's own program pages currently 404/moved, so the $100K/24mo details rest on Techstars' official page (high) rather than Google's current terms (could not be read today). Marketplace/SaaS add-ons and support fees are typically excluded from GCP startup credits; overage beyond credits is billed.
  - *Source:* https://www.techstars.com/capital;https://cloud.google.com/startups
- **AWS + Techstars Impact Bootcamps (free for underrepresented founders, incl. students/early founders)** (medium) — Free 2-day bootcamps (launched 2025, 6 cities: Atlanta, New York, Mexico City, London in 2025; Tokyo, Berlin in early 2026) managed locally by Techstars: hands-on AWS technical training, business coaching, mentors/investors. Each participating founder gets $5,000 in AWS promotional credits. Best-in-class startups are invited to the AWS Founder Retreat — a weekend with in-person investor meetings and the chance to win up to $50,000 in unrestricted grant funding plus an additional $15,000 in AWS promotional credits. Top participants considered for Techstars accelerator or Founder Catalyst programs. Part of AWS's $30M commitment (announced 2022) to Black, Women, Latino and LGBTQIA+ founders.
  - *Eligibility:* Founding teams must identify as a member of an underrepresented community (Black, Women, Latino, Indigenous, LGBTQIA+, founders with disabilities) or demonstrate work/engagement supporting such communities; self-funded, angel-backed, or raised up to Series A; clear need for cloud technology; interest in scaling/fundraising. No .edu email requirement; free to attend (event slots filled by application).
  - *How to claim:* Apply via the AWS Impact Bootcamps page (https://aws.amazon.com/startups/lp/aws-impact-bootcamps) for one of the six city cohorts; attend the 2-day workshop; receive $5,000 AWS credits as a participant; winners advance to the AWS Founder Retreat for the up-to-$50K grant + $15K credits and Techstars pipeline.
  - *Expiry:* Event-driven (2025-2026 city calendar); the $5,000 promotional credits follow AWS promotional-credit terms (short validity, no extensions). No standing free tier — only while a cohort is running.
  - *Gotchas:* The $50K + $15K prizes go only to 'best-in-class' retreat participants, not all attendees. Bootcamp pages may be city-specific and time-boxed. 'Free' = free workshop + credits, but the AWS credits require a paid-plan AWS account to activate (Free-plan accounts are ineligible).
  - *Source:* https://press.aboutamazon.com/aws/2025/7/aws-and-techstars-launch-impact-bootcamps-to-support-underrepresented-founders-globally;https://aws.amazon.com/startups/lp/aws-impact-bootcamps
- **Techstars Founder Catalyst (free pre-accelerator, no equity)** (high) — 10-week part-time, primarily virtual pre-accelerator for early-stage/idea-stage founders: training, tools, mentorship, cohort community, and network access. 'There is no equity exchange in Founder Catalyst programs.' No tuition listed. Current/planned editions include Techstars x EIT Urban Mobility Founder Catalyst, Alabama, and Global Fall 2026. Complements Techstars' 'Startup Weekend' community events (paid event series) and the University Catalyst pipeline.
  - *Eligibility:* Pre-funding or idea-stage founders who have raised less than $400K, possess domain expertise in the problem space, have a defined customer need with a lead on a solution, and collaborative spirit. No student/.edu requirement — open to any early founder. Free to join; no equity.
  - *How to claim:* Sign up via the Airtable 'Hear About Future Programs' form on the Techstars Founder Catalyst page or through apply.techstars.com; selected cohorts run 10 weeks; strong participants can feed into Techstars accelerators.
  - *Expiry:* Cohort-based, rolling; specific editions (EIT/Alabama/Global Fall 2026) have their own application windows; benefit access is not perpetual like accelerator network access.
  - *Gotchas:* Not a funding program — no capital invested (the $220K deal requires actual accelerator acceptance). Some editions are co-branded (EIT, University of Alabama) and may carry local eligibility emphasis.
  - *Source:* https://www.techstars.com/founder-catalyst
- **Techstars University Catalyst / university-partnered accelerators (student & alumni pipeline)** (medium) — Techstars University Catalyst (with USC) targets the student/alumni pipeline, referenced on Techstars' own pages with a 2026 class in progress; 'USC and Techstars Accelerator' is an official accelerator in the Spring 2026 cycle (alongside Johns Hopkins/CareFirst AI Health Baltimore, Northwestern Medicine, Columbus/Ohio State, etc.). Accepted university-partnered programs receive the same $220K structure (or APAC $120K where applicable) plus the $1M+ credit package. No separate student-only free tier or education license exists at Techstars itself.
  - *Eligibility:* Students/alumni via University Catalyst (pipeline, free-to-attend format); accelerator acceptance through the same application as other programs. No .edu email requirement for the investment itself.
  - *How to claim:* Attend the University Catalyst cohort offered at your university (USC partnership), or apply to a university-branded accelerator (e.g., USC and Techstars Accelerator, application window closed Nov 19, 2025 for Spring 2026 per Techstars' blog) via apply.techstars.com.
  - *Expiry:* Cohort-based; new university partnerships announced per year (March 2026 update lists active editions).
  - *Gotchas:* University-branded cohorts are still funded on the standard accelerator terms — the university is a partner, not the investor; expect the same side-letter obligations. Student-facing 'free' elements are the pipeline events, not the $220K deal.
  - *Source:* https://www.techstars.com/founder-catalyst;https://www.techstars.com/blog/program-news/applications-are-now-open-for-techstars-spring-2026-accelerator-programs
- **2025–26 change:** Changes/sunsets in 2025-2026: (1) April 17, 2025 — Techstars replaced its $120K deal ($100K MFN SAFE + $20K/5%) with the $220K deal ($200K uncapped MFN SAFE + $20K post-money CEA/5%) for Fall 2025 cohorts onward, explicitly mirroring YC; the $120K structure now applies only to Asia-Pacific programs. (2) The ARM Labs Lagos Techstars accelerator (Techstars' Africa-specific program, launched Dec 2022, 2 cohorts) closed in late 2024 as part of a global restructuring; African founders can still apply to global programs. (3) No Techstars program was sunset in 2025-2026 that would reduce the credit stack — DigitalOcean Startups was upgraded to 2026 'Premier Partner' (announced March 2026), expanding rather than reducing access. (4) Verification gaps today (2026-10-22): the AWS Techstars partnership page (aws.amazon.com/startups/partnerships/techstars → startups.aws.com/...), DigitalOcean's partner/startup pages (digitalocean.com/partners/startups, /startup-cloud), and Google's startup program pages (cloud.google.com/launch, /application/startup-faqs) all return 404, so AWS/DO/GCP per-credit-line details above rely on Techstars' official /capital page (read today) plus partner-program public docs; treat exact $100K/24-month figures as Techstars-stated until re-confirmed against the partners' current portals. (5) There is no Techstars student free tier, trial-credit self-serve offer, or education license (no .edu requirement anywhere in the funnel); student access is via University Catalyst and the free AWS Impact Bootcamps.

### Y Combinator

- **YC AI Stack (a.k.a. YC AI Student Starter Pack) — student program** (high) — Bundle of free credits from ~20 partners for students who attend a YC university event (in person, starting Fall 2025). YC's official blog (Jan 16, 2026) headlines it as 'over $20,000 in cloud credits on Azure and AWS, over $5,000 in credits for GPT/Claude/Grok, plus generous credits for AI devtools' (14 YC companies), marketed as 'over $25,000' total. The live deals page deals.ycombinator.com/students (read 2026-10-22) currently names only AWS $10,000, OpenAI $1,000, Anthropic $500 plus 17 devtool logos; earlier 2026 snapshots of the same page also listed Azure $10,000, Grok (xAI) $2,500 and Cursor $720. Per-provider amounts are still fluctuating as YC 'rolls this out.'
  - *Eligibility:* Students who attend a YC university event starting Fall 2025 (in person). No startup, company or funding requirement. No .edu-domain check and no credit card required per the page. Access auto-detected via the Hacker News account the student used to RSVP.
  - *How to claim:* 1) Attend a YC university event and RSVP via Hacker News account; 2) receive the confirmation email after the event; 3) sign in at https://deals.ycombinator.com/students with the same Hacker News account; 4) browse and redeem each partner deal individually (each runs its own redemption flow).
  - *Expiry:* Access lasts one year from the date of the qualifying event. Each partner's credits carry their own expiry. One third-party guide (blurredai, Feb 2026) says the email must be re-verified every 30 days to keep access.
  - *Gotchas:* Live-page named amounts are LOWER than the Jan 2026 blog headline ('$20K+ cloud / $5K+ GPT-Claude-Grok') and the page is explicitly still expanding, so exact credit values vary by event/cohort. Devtool per-tool dollar caps are not published. Only available where YC actually hosts university events (regional/US-centric). No .edu gate is stated, so it is event-attendance-gated rather than verified-student-gated. Secondary claim URL: https://deals.ycombinator.com/students
  - *Source:* https://www.ycombinator.com/blog/the-yc-ai-student-starter-pack/
- **YC Startup Credits Program (VC-funded umbrella for YC batch companies)** (high) — YC's official apply page states 'Each YC company gets access to $12M+ in free credits and deals from top partners, including OpenAI, Anthropic, AWS, Google Cloud, Azure, Stripe, and a long list of others.' This is the accelerator-funded stack delivered to accepted YC companies during/after the batch; a single company's realizable value is far below the $12M marketing total (roughly the ~$350K+ multi-partner stack in the task's 'known so far').
  - *Eligibility:* Must be accepted into a YC batch (YC invests when a company is accepted, not when the batch starts). No public funding-age cap on the umbrella itself, but each partner program below imposes its own caps (e.g. AWS pre-Series B, <10 yrs old).
  - *How to claim:* Be a YC batch company -> receive partner Org IDs / promo codes during YC onboarding -> apply through each partner's portal -> redeem the YC-partnered deals through the sign-in-gated deals portal (account.ycombinator.com / YC company account at deals.ycombinator.com).
  - *Expiry:* Varies per partner program (see individual partner findings below).
  - *Gotchas:* '$12M+' is YC's aggregate marketing figure across all partners, not what one company gets. The full deals catalog is sign-in-gated, so individual YC-partner amounts are not publicly itemized on YC's own site; per-company value depends on which partner tiers you qualify for.
  - *Source:* https://www.ycombinator.com/apply
- **AWS Activate credits for YC startups** (medium) — Up to ~$500,000 in AWS Activate credits for eligible YC batch companies (the $500K level was confirmed for the W24 cohort), typically split ~$200K general cloud + ~$300K AI silicon (Trainium/Inferentia, Amazon Bedrock incl. third-party FMs, reserved H100). The public AWS Activate Portfolio tier caps at $200K; the $500K is the YC-negotiated tier. As of 11 May 2026 AWS Activate credits became redeemable for third-party models on Amazon Bedrock.
  - *Eligibility:* Active or very recent YC batch company (W24 onward at the $500K tier); company <10 years old; pre-Series B or self-funded; valid company website plus matching business-email domain; cannot have already received AWS Activate Portfolio credits of equal or greater value.
  - *How to claim:* Receive the YC Org ID from YC onboarding (Y Combinator is a designated AWS Activate Provider) -> create/sign in an AWS Builder ID -> apply for Activate Portfolio with the YC Org ID -> link your AWS account; expect a ~5-10 business day review.
  - *Expiry:* 2 years from the moment AWS activates the credits on the account; no extensions permitted.
  - *Gotchas:* Credit card MUST be on file (no charge while within credits, but overages bill with no hard stop). Credits are non-transferable and locked to one AWS billing org; AWS auto-applies them; excluded spend includes AWS Professional/Managed Services, Mechanical Turk, crypto-mining, Route 53 domain fees and upfront RI/Savings Plan fees. The 2-year clock starts at activation, not Demo Day, so applying early in the batch preserves more usable time. YC Startup School / older alumni / rejected applicants get only the standard Portfolio tier (up to $200K), not the $500K.
  - *Source:* https://aws.amazon.com/startups/credits/
- **OpenAI API credits for YC startups** (medium) — OpenAI overhauled its YC startup program in 2026: up to $500,000 in API credits with NO equity requirement, plus an optional ~$1.5M additional tranche in exchange for equity stakes. In May 2026 (Spring 2026 batch, ~169 companies) Sam Altman announced $2M in OpenAI API credits per startup via uncapped SAFE agreements (~$338M total for that batch). Per WSJ-quoted reporting, the two firms could together hand out up to $800M/yr to YC startups.
  - *Eligibility:* YC-backed / YC batch startups. The $500K tier has no equity requirement; the $1.5M and $2M amounts require signing equity (SAFE) agreements.
  - *How to claim:* Arranged through YC's partnership with OpenAI and distributed via YC onboarding / the YC deals portal, not a self-serve public application.
  - *Expiry:* Not publicly stated; credits are redeemable exclusively with OpenAI (intentional vendor lock-in).
  - *Gotchas:* A large share of the headline value is equity-funded (SAFE/equity), so it is not 'free' — it is equity-for-credits. Credits usable only on OpenAI models. The structure changed materially in 2026 (baseline offers were far smaller, ~$30K, in earlier years).
  - *Source:* https://mlq.ai/news/openai-and-anthropic-pour-up-to-800m-a-year-in-free-credits-into-yc-startups/
- **Anthropic (Claude) credits for YC startups** (medium) — Anthropic raised its standard YC credit offer in 2026 from $30,000 to $500,000 per YC startup, with NO equity requirement — explicitly pitched as the founder-friendly counter to OpenAI's equity-based offer (a ~16x increase).
  - *Eligibility:* YC-backed / YC batch startups. No equity requirement for the $500K tier.
  - *How to claim:* Arranged through YC's partnership with Anthropic and distributed via YC onboarding / the YC deals portal.
  - *Expiry:* Not publicly stated; usable on the Anthropic API (and, for some stacks, via AWS Bedrock / GCP Vertex AI where Anthropic models are hosted).
  - *Gotchas:* The jump from $30K to $500K happened in 2026. Figure is from WSJ-quoted press coverage, not read on Anthropic's public page today; actual per-startup allocation may vary by negotiation.
  - *Source:* https://the-decoder.com/openai-and-anthropic-are-giving-away-millions-in-computing-power-to-attract-startups/
- **Google Cloud / Gemini credits for YC startups** (medium) — Google Cloud is now handing out up to $500,000 in GCP credits to YC startups, plus early access to new Gemini models and occasional access to DeepMind engineers. The public 'Google Cloud for Startups' program generally caps around $200K; the $500K is the YC-negotiated level.
  - *Eligibility:* YC-backed / YC batch startups; standard GCP-for-Startups eligibility otherwise (early-stage, technology-based).
  - *How to claim:* Applied via YC's Google Cloud partnership; GCP for Startups application with YC as the referring provider.
  - *Expiry:* GCP startup credits are typically good for ~12 months.
  - *Gotchas:* The $500K YC figure is from a Google spokesperson quoted in WSJ-press, not from YC or Google's public page today; early Gemini access / DeepMind engineer time are not guaranteed entitlements.
  - *Source:* https://the-decoder.com/openai-and-anthropic-are-giving-away-millions-in-computing-power-to-attract-startups/
- **Microsoft Azure credits for YC startups** (low) ⚠ low confidence: verify before claiming — Microsoft for Startups (Founders Hub) offers up to ~$150,000 in Azure credits unlocked across milestone tiers, plus free GitHub Enterprise, Visual Studio and Microsoft 365, with Azure OpenAI model access included. YC founders commonly reach it through YC's partnership or via the public Founders Hub.
  - *Eligibility:* Startups (self-funded or VC-backed; no equity required); YC batch companies can access Azure credits through the YC partnership or apply directly to Founders Hub.
  - *How to claim:* Apply through Microsoft for Startups Founders Hub, or receive Azure credits via YC partner onboarding.
  - *Expiry:* Azure credits are generally valid ~12 months; further tiers unlock by meeting product/milestones rather than auto-renewing.
  - *Gotchas:* Credits are 'up to $150K' and gated behind milestone achievements, so most startups land well below the cap. Sourced from a third-party 2025 comparison guide, not read on Azure/Founders Hub today — lowest confidence of the partner programs.
  - *Source:* https://firstday.dev/blog/cloud-credits-comparison-2025
- **YC AI devtools bundle (voice / browser / search / video / DB)** (medium) — 14-17 YC devtool companies provide free AI devtool credits bundled into both the YC AI Stack (students) and the YC startup deals portal: Firecrawl, Exa, Vapi, Browser Use, Reducto, Gumloop, Deepgram, Greptile, Supabase, AgentMail, Langfuse, sync., Coinbase, Roboflow, Tavus, Blaxel, Bolna AI, Respan and Cursor ($720 in one student snapshot). These cover voice AI, browser automation, web search, video generation, document parsing and databases. Per-tool dollar caps are not published on the YC page.
  - *Eligibility:* Students via the YC AI Stack (attending a YC university event); startups via being a YC batch company / deals portal.
  - *How to claim:* Sign in to https://deals.ycombinator.com/students (students) or the YC deals portal (startups), then redeem each devtool deal individually through that company's own signup flow.
  - *Expiry:* Varies per tool; YC does not publish per-tool expiry dates.
  - *Gotchas:* Per-tool limits are hidden and the partner roster/changelogs shift as YC expands the stack; some entries are trials rather than perpetual free tiers, and each carries its own terms and expiry.
  - *Source:* https://deals.ycombinator.com/students
- **2025–26 change:** 2025: YC reportedly REMOVED the public "YC Startup School Deals & Offers" page (per startcamp.com, Feb 2025). Startup deals moved behind sign-in at deals.ycombinator.com, so YC Startup School members no longer automatically get the old public AWS $5K (non-China) / $1,500+3K rebate (China) tier; that older startup-school AWS deal is effectively retired. 2025-2026 partner CREDITS CHANGES (all EXPANSIONS, no partner program was sunset): (a) Anthropic raised its YC offer from $30K to $500K, no equity; (b) OpenAI overhauled to $500K no-equity + optional $1.5M equity tranche, and offered $2M via uncapped SAFE to the entire Spring 2026 batch (May 2026); (c) Google Cloud up to $500K + early Gemini access; (d) YC's apply-page headline raised to "$12M+ in free credits"; (e) as of 11 May 2026 AWS extended Activate credits to be redeemable for third-party models on Amazon Bedrock (previously credits could not cover third-party AI model usage). Student PROGRAM CHANGES: the YC AI Student Starter Pack was rebranded the "YC AI Stack" and is being 'rolled out' to more students in 2026; the live deals page's named credit amounts have rotated down from the Jan 2026 blog headline ($20K+ cloud / $5K+ GPT-Claude-Grok to currently ~$10K AWS + $1K OpenAI + $500 Anthropic + devtools), so the per-provider figures are moving. No genuine YC-branded free-tier or education-LICENSE program (in the software sense) exists — YC is a VC/accelerator, so all 'offers' are partner credit bundles delivered through YC.
